======================================================================
BIOLOGY KNOWLEDGE BASE
======================================================================

Entry 1
Category: Cell Biology
Concept: Biology
Definition: The scientific study of living organisms and life processes.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in biology.
- Important in medicine, biotechnology and environmental science.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Biology is an important topic in biology.
Related Concepts:
Cells, Genetics, Human Anatomy, Ecology, Evolution, Microbiology

Entry 2
Category: Cell Biology
Concept: Cell
Definition: The basic structural and functional unit of life.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in biology.
- Important in medicine, biotechnology and environmental science.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
A human body contains trillions of cells.

Cells, Genetics, Human Anatomy, Ecology, Evolution, Microbiology

Entry 40
Category: Ecology & Microbiology
Concept: Photosynthesis

- Fundamental concept in biology.
- Important in medicine, biotechnology and environmental science.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
6CO₂ + 6H₂O → C₆H₁₂O₆ + 6O₂

Category: Ecology & Microbiology

Category: Cell Biology
Concept: Biology
Definition: The scientific study of living organisms and life processes.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in biology.
- Important in medicine, biotechnology and environmental science.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Biology is an important topic in biology.

Cells, Genetics, Human Anatomy, Ecology, Evolution, Microbiology
